Writes a verified:false distillation note to 08_Insights/ with sources preserved verbatim. Use to compress a long lesson/topic into traceable facts. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/distill.md) · ★2)
- **sb:emerge**: Surface emergent patterns across lessons/zettels/topics — clusters notes by shared tag or repeated title token and drafts a verified:false synthesis page in 08_Insights/ for each cluster of 3+ notes lacking one. Dry-run by default; --apply writes. Use to compound scattered notes into higher-order insights. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/emerge.md) · ★2)
- **sb:eval**: Measure retrieval quality of the sb lexical retriever — recall@1/3/5/10 + MRR per note-type over a held-out, Haiku-paraphrased question set. Use to check whether /sb:ask, /sb:recall, and /sb:search are actually surfacing the right notes. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/eval.md) · ★2)
- **sb:export**: Bundle a topic + all its tagged + wikilinked notes into a single markdown file under `exports/` for sharing. Usage — `/sb:export &lt;topic-slug&gt;` or `/sb:export "#area/sub"`. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/export.md) · ★2)
- **sb:find**: Fuzzy filename/title lookup across the vault. Usage — `/sb:find &lt;fragment&gt;`. Fast, no LLM. For full-text content search use /sb:search; for Q&A use /sb:ask.
